國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
內需與房市提供支撐 美國財政及通膨風險升高 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額為 7,450 億美元 ， 月增0.8%、 年增6.3%；
扣除汽車及零件銷售後月增 0.9%，進一步扣除汽車、零件及加油站後
之核心零售銷售月增 0.2%。加油站、餐飲及非實體零售銷售持續成長，
顯示美國消費需求仍具韌性，惟成長動能較前月略有放緩。 
⚫ 2026 財年美國聯邦政府財政赤字達 1 兆 9,924.7 億美元，年增 12.2%，
主因政府支出成長高於收入。9 月單月由去年同期盈餘轉為 269 億美元
赤字，關稅退稅增加壓縮收入，而全年淨利息支出突破1 兆美元、年增
8.4%。在聯邦債務總額逾 40 兆美元下，財政赤字擴大及債務融資成本
攀升，持續考驗財政永續性。 
⚫ 9 月美國成屋銷售年率 399 萬戶，月增 1.5%、年減 2.2%；成屋中位數
售價 41.75 萬美元，年增 1.3%，連續 39 個月上漲。待售庫存增至 160
萬戶 、 供給量4.8 個月 ， 顯示供給改善 ， 惟高利率環境仍抑制購屋需求 。 
⚫ 9 月美國消費者物價指數 (CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.6%，高於 8 月之
0.4%，年增率由 3.4%升至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，年增率由 2.4%
升至 2.6%。受能源價格上揚影響，能源指數月增 5.2%，其中汽油價格
大幅上漲，成為推升通膨之主要因素；住房指數續增0.4%。整體而言，
美國通膨壓力較前月升溫，核心物價漲勢亦略有增強。 
⚫ 9 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)月增 1.3%，年增率由 5.6%升至 6.3%；能
源價格大漲 10.8%為主要推升因素 ， 帶動商品價格月增2.6%。 服務價格
亦月增 0.8%，核心 PPI 年增率由 4.8%升至 5.0%，顯示生產端通膨壓力
持續升高。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月中旬，多位聯準會官員表示通膨仍具壓力。Mary Daly 認為未來可
能仍需升息，但 10 月底會議無迫切行動必要；Beth Hammack 警告若通
膨預期惡化，可能加大緊縮力道；主席 Kevin Warsh 則指出美國經濟仍
具韌性，但能源價格上漲及 AI 投資擴張恐推升通膨壓力。整體而言，
聯準會立場偏鷹，但短期升息時點仍視通膨走勢而定。 
⚫ 美墨關稅談判原接近達成初步協議 ， 惟美國商務部長反對調降汽車及鋼
鋁關稅，使協商進展受阻。墨西哥則爭取降低汽車關稅及擴大零組件抵

事件

將賦予歐盟在極端情況下 ， 迅速且全面封鎖特定國家企業進入歐洲單一
市場的權限。

每年 35 萬輛的配額上限(每年可遞增 6%)。 
短評 
⚫ 雖德國財政擴張有助支持工業生產，但法國政治、選舉等不利前景重挫
市場信心，恐會影響企業以及政府在各項支出、投資決策，進而形成拖
累因素。 
⚫ 歐盟的 「下一代歐盟復甦計畫」(NextGenerationEU)將在年底前完成所有
撥款，後續須關注是否有其他替代財政工具來緩解財政真空 (近期歐盟
領袖峰會雖有協商相關議題但無法取得共識)，以繼續支撐景氣動能。

成長。

重要 
事件 
⚫ 歐中貿易談判公布相關細節 ， 未來中國出口至歐盟的混合動力汽車設定
每年 35 萬輛的配額上限(每年可遞增 6%)。依照歐盟推算，中國對歐盟
的混合動力車出口量將較原先潛在規模減少一半以上。 
短評 
⚫ 雖歐中貿易協議不利於汽車出口 ， 但有助於避免歐盟進一步擴大貿易保

等方式因應配額限制，藉此維持在歐洲市場的競爭力與市占率。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.19 
一、 美國—內需與房市提供支撐 美國財政及通膨風險升高 
2026 年 9 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額經季節性調整後為 7,450 億美元，較
上月增加 0.8%，年增 6.3%。若扣除汽車及零件銷售，零售額月增 0.9%；進一步
扣除汽車、零件及加油站後，核心零售銷售月增0.2%，顯示整體消費需求仍維持
成長。從主要業別表現觀察，加油站、非實體零售商及餐飲服務與飲酒場所銷售
額均較上月增加，其中加油站及餐飲消費表現相對亮眼，電子及家電商店銷售亦
呈成長。整體而言，9 月美國零售銷售延續擴張走勢，顯示民間消費仍具支撐力
道，且多數主要消費類別維持正成長，惟核心零售增幅較前月放緩，後續仍須關
注物價、就業及金融情勢變化對消費支出的影響。 
2026 年 9 月(2026 財年第 12 個月)美國聯邦政府財政赤字為 269.0 億美元 ， 較
去年同期 1,979.4 億美元財政盈餘轉為赤字。當月政府收入 5,578.8 億美元，年增
2.6%；政府支出 5,847.8 億美元，年增 69.1%，支出增幅明顯高於收入。收入方
面，9 月關稅淨收入由 8 月的 128.4 億美元降至 59.1 億美元，累計2026 財年關稅
淨收入約 1,732.2 億美元，年減11.1%，關稅退稅增加已部分抵銷關稅收入成長。
支出方面，9 月聯邦政府淨利息支出約 347.9 億美元，全年累計達1 兆 517.5 億美
元 ， 年增8.4%， 反映高利率環境下債務利息負擔持續攀升 。 累計2026 財年全年 ，
美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1 兆 9,924.7 億美元，較 2025 財年擴大 12.2%。其中，
政府收入年增 3.2%至 5 兆 4,033.3 億美元 ， 支出年增5.5%至 7 兆 3,958.0 億美元 ，
顯示支出成長仍快於收入增加。從支出結構觀察，社會安全、醫療保險、國防及
淨利息支出均較上年增加，持續推升財政壓力。整體而言，美國財政赤字擴大主
要反映政府支出成長強勁、利息負擔居高不下，以及關稅退稅增加削弱部分收入
效果 。 在聯邦債務總額突破40 兆美元背景下 ， 未來財政赤字走勢 、 債務融資成本
及財政永續性問題，仍將是市場關注的重要焦點。 
2026 年 9 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率升至 399 萬戶 ， 較上月增加1.5%，
惟較去年同期減少 2.2%，整體房市成交動能仍偏疲弱。區域表現方面，南部及西
部分別月增 2.8%，中西部持平，東北部月減 2.1%，四大地區銷售量均較去年同
期下滑。房價方面，成屋售價中位數為 41.75 萬美元，年增 1.3%，已連續 39 個

月維持年增走勢。供給端則持續增加，待售成屋庫存升至 160 萬戶，較上月增加
1.9%， 較去年同期增加4.6%， 相當於4.8 個月供給量 ， 高於去年同期的4.5 個月 。
全美住房可負擔指數由去年同期 106.7 降至 105.4， 顯示購屋負擔仍受房價及利率
影響。整體而言，9 月美國成屋市場呈現成交量略有回升、房價維持溫和漲勢及
待售庫存持續累積的格局，顯示供給條件改善，但高利率環境仍對購屋需求形成
壓力。 
2026 年 9 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.6%，高於上月之
0.4%，年增率由 3.4%升至 3.7%；扣除食品與能源之核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，與上月
持平，年增率則由 2.4%升至 2.6%。在細項方面，能源價格持續上揚，能源指數
月增 5.2%，其中汽油價格大幅上漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅逾半，為本月通
膨升溫主因；住房指數月增 0.4%，住房項目價格漲幅尚未明顯趨緩。食品指數則
持平，其中外食價格月增0.2%，家庭食品價格月減 0.1%。此外，二手車及卡車、
機票、休閒娛樂及服飾等價格上漲，通訊價格則下跌。整體而言，9 月美國通膨
明顯回升，主要受汽油價格大幅上漲及住房成本續增帶動；核心 CPI 月增率維持
0.3%，年增率則回升至 2.6%，顯示核心物價壓力較前月略有增強，未來通膨走勢
仍須持續關注能源價格變化及住房等服務項目價格表現。 
 2026 年 9 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 1.3%，高於上月之
0.5%，年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%。其中，最終需求商品價格月增 2.6%，主要受
能源價格月增 10.8%帶動，以汽油、柴油及航空燃油價格漲幅較為顯著；食品價
格亦月增 1.2%。另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.8%，主要反映批發及零售
利潤率、運輸服務價格上升。剔除食品、能源及貿易服務後之核心最終需求 PPI
月增 0.5%，高於上月之 0.3%，年增率由 4.8%升至 5.0%。整體而言，9 月 PPI 升
幅明顯擴大，主要反映能源與服務價格同步上揚，顯示生產端成本壓力仍偏高，
後續仍須關注能源價格及運輸成本變化對通膨走勢之影響。 
10 月中旬，多位聯準會官員針對貨幣政策發表看法。舊金山聯準銀行總裁戴
利(Mary Daly) 表示，雖然通膨仍高於 2%目標，未來可能仍需進一步升息，但目
前無迫切必要在 10 月 27 日至 28 日 FOMC 會議立即採取行動，顯示聯準會傾向
先觀察後續數據表現。克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪克(Beth Hammack)則警告，若
通膨預期持續惡化，聯準會可能必須採取更積極的緊縮措施，以維持物價穩定。
聯準會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)10 月 16 日則表示，美國經濟受穩健就業市場及 AI

投資熱潮支撐而維持韌性，但能源價格上漲及 AI 投資擴張恐推升通膨壓力，並
未對下次利率決策預作表態 。 整體而言 ， 聯準會官員發言顯示政策立場仍偏鷹派 ，
但對短期升息時點保持彈性，未來通膨及通膨預期變化仍是利率決策的重要依據。 
近期美墨貿易談判再度受到關稅爭議影響。據《華爾街日報》報導，雙方原
已接近達成初步協議 ， 美國擬調降對墨西哥汽車 、 鋼鐵及鋁製品的部分進口關稅 ，
以換取墨西哥在貿易政策、商品採購及其他經貿議題上的讓步，並為後續《美墨
加協定》 （USMCA） 檢討與延長協商創造有利條件 。 然而 ， 美國商務部長Howard 
Lutnick 反對大幅調降目前對墨西哥汽車課徵的 25%關稅 ， 以及鋼鐵 、 鋁製品與部
分衍生製成品的 50%關稅，使談判進展受阻。墨西哥方面則希望將汽車關稅降至
15%或以下，以縮小與日本、南韓汽車適用稅率的差距，並擴大汽車關稅抵減機
制對美墨加三國零組件價值的認列範圍；惟美方仍須考量國內金屬產業對降稅可
能衝擊本土生產及就業的疑慮。儘管雙方對關稅調整幅度仍存分歧，墨西哥經貿
官員表示協商已有部分進展，美國商務部亦強調持續與墨方保持溝通，顯示談判
尚未正式破局。整體而言，美墨關稅協商結果不僅攸關北美汽車及金屬產業供應
鏈成本與競爭力，也可能影響 USMCA 後續檢討與延長協商進程；若雙方遲遲無
法縮小分歧，恐增加企業投資與供應鏈布局的不確定性，並對北美區域貿易整合
形成壓力。 
二、歐元區—工業生產走疲，前景有待觀察 
 歐元區 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)較 7 月下滑 0.5%，呈現連續三個月下降，
其中非耐久消費財、中間財及能源產出分別減少 2.7%、0.8%、0.6%，僅資本財、
耐久消費財產出分別成長 0.4%、0.2%。儘管德國 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)反彈
0.4%，係受惠車廠陸續結束歲修，但法、義、西工業生產分別下跌0.3%、1.3%、
0.8%為主要拖累因素，反映先前美伊戰爭帶來的庫存儲備需求逐漸消退，尤其以
能源密集產業(化工、造紙、 鋼鐵等)變化最為明顯。整體而言，即便排除能源密集
產業的波動，其他製造業諸如製藥、汽車、機械等生產情形皆偏疲軟。 
 展望未來，近期德國在財政擴張的支持下，工廠訂單大幅成長，未來可望陸
續反映在該國工業生產表現；但法國政治、選舉等不利前景重挫市場信心，恐會
影響企業以及政府在各項支出 、 投資決策 ， 進而形成拖累因素 。 另外 ， 歐盟的 「下
一代歐盟復甦計畫」(NextGenerationEU)已在 9 月底截止撥款申請 ， 並將在年底前

三、日本—AI 投資續強，帶動機械訂單穩健復甦 
 8 月核心機械訂單(扣除波動訂單)月增 5.2%，其中製造業訂單月增 1.6%，人
工智慧相關投資仍是主要驅動力，資通訊產業訂單月增 13.0%，有色金屬也大幅

7 月大幅成長後出現回落；零售業則在連續三個月下滑後月增 29.2%。 整體而言 ，
機械訂單數據顯示企業資本支出呈現更廣泛且穩固的復甦趨勢，尤以 AI 相關投
資為主要支撐。 
四、中國—AI 相關需求帶動出口動能強勁成長 
9 月出口年增率較 8 月大幅增加 11.3 個百分點至 36.3%，出口總值為 4,472
億美元 ； 累計前九個月出口年增率上升為21.3%， 累積出口總值為33,707 億美元 。
前九個月出口主要成長項目(累積年增率)包含 IC(108.1%)、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等，54.6%)、船舶(29.9%)、汽車(54.3%)，其中 IC 及自動數據處理設備
出口加速成長，為 9 月出口年增率大幅上升的主要因素。 
9 月進口年增率較 8 月增 2.8 個百分點至 31%，進口總值為 3,137 億美元；累
計前九個月進口值成長 27.5%至 24,326 億美元。其中 IC、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等)進口分別成長 63.5%、124.8%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部
分伺服器關鍵組件有賴於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前九個月貿易順差達
9,381 億美元，僅較去年同期增加 7.7%，增幅明顯低於出口，顯示出口擴張同時
伴隨關鍵原物料進口大幅增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價
值產品依賴國際供應鏈支持。 
9 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 8 月的 3.8%升至 5%， 主因原油價格自7 月起走
升，其影響逐漸反映於上游生產成本。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格年增率
續揚 14.1 個百分點至 24.5%，並帶動燃料加工業出廠價格年增率上揚至 21.6%，
此外，對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學品、化纖出廠價格年增率加速升至
12.1%、12.3%，顯示原油價格上漲的影響開始擴散至中下游產業。此外，由於近
期鋼鐵價格走弱 ， 黑色金屬礦產開採 、 冶煉加工業出廠價格分別年減3.4%、0.2%，

若油價維持高檔或進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。 
展望未來，歐中談判已就汽車貿易達成部分共識，包含未來中國出口至歐盟
的混合動力汽車設定每年 35 萬輛的配額上限，且配額規模可逐年增加 6%。依照
歐盟預估 2026 年自中國進口約 80 萬輛混合動力汽車推算，中國對歐盟的混合動
力車出口量將較原先潛在規模減少一半以上。雖該協議不利於汽車出口，但有助
於避免歐盟進一步擴大貿易保護措施，中國車廠則可能會加速透過赴歐投資設
廠、提升當地生產比重等方式因應配額限制，藉此維持在歐洲市場的競爭力與市
占率。

下行風險，建議各國政府提升農業生產效率及糧食安全，提升數位經濟及服務業
競爭力，強化財政紀律並提高公共投資效率，維持穩健貨幣政策與匯率穩定。

六、台灣—AI 帶動出口、投資與消費成長，中經院上修 2026 年經濟預測 
中經院 10 月 16 日公布最新預測，上修 2026 年台灣經濟成長率至 12.16%，
創 1988 年以來新高，主要受惠於 AI 技術快速發展帶動全球科技投資，半導體與
資通訊產品需求強勁，出口維持高速成長，同時民間投資與消費亦同步擴張，經
濟成長動能由內、外需共同支撐。另一方面，由於中東地緣政治風險升溫帶動油
價維持高檔，能源價格外溢效果推升整體物價，中經院將 2026 年 CPI 年增率預
估上修至 2.06%，惟整體通膨仍屬相對溫和。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國財政壓力仍高，房市降溫與通膨回升增添經濟不確定性 
經濟 
⚫ 8 月美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1,668 億美元，年減 51.6%，主因收入增
加、支出下降，但部分支出提前至7 月支付，影響單月表現。累計前11
個月赤字仍近 2 兆美元，關稅收入轉正，惟利息支出持續攀升，財政壓
力仍高。 
⚫ 8 月美國成屋銷售年率降至 398 萬戶，月減 2.0%、年減 1.2%，為 2025
年 6 月以來首度跌破 400 萬戶，顯示高房貸利率持續抑制房市需求。房
價中位數為 42.91 萬美元，年增 1.6%，連續 38 個月上漲；待售成屋庫
存增至 162 萬戶，相當於 4.9 個月供給量，創逾 10 年高點。整體而言，
成屋市場呈現銷售放緩、房價續升與庫存增加態勢，供給改善有助市場
逐步趨於平衡。 
⚫ 8 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%，高於上月 0.1%，年增率維持
3.4%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，年增率則降至 2.4%。能源價格由跌轉升，
其中汽油上漲 3.9%， 住房月增0.3%， 為推升通膨主因 ； 食品月增0.1%。
整體而言，短期物價壓力升溫，但核心通膨仍持續降溫。 
⚫ 8 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)月增 0.4%、年增 5.4%，高於上月的 0.1%
及 4.8%，主要反映能源價格回升影響，其中柴油價格大漲24.1%；服務
價格則受運輸及倉儲成本增加推升而續呈上揚。核心 PPI 月增 0.3%，
低於前月的 0.4%，顯示生產端通膨壓力仍具韌性，惟基礎通膨動能尚
未明顯升溫。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期美加貿易衝突持續升溫 ， 加拿大自9 月 8 日起對美國商品加徵 15%
至 50%報復性關稅 ；美國則擴大限制部分加拿大乳製品 、酒類及機車產
品輸美，並限制加拿大企業參與部分政府採購，雙方爭端已由關稅戰延
伸至市場准入領域，增添北美供應鏈及貿易前景不確定性。 
⚫ 美國國會推進對俄羅斯新制裁法案 ， 擬授權總統對俄羅斯能源主要購買

一、 美國—財政壓力仍高，房市降溫與通膨回升增添經濟不確定性 
2026 年 8 月(2026 財年第 11 個月)美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1,668.0 億美元，
較去年同期 3,447.9 億美元縮減 51.6%；當月政府收入約 3,600.3 億美元，年增
4.6%，政府支出約 5,268.3 億美元，年減23.5%，惟部分原定 8 月初支付的軍人薪
資與退休金、退伍軍人福利、補充保障收入及部分醫療保險給付款項因 8 月 1 日
適逢非工作日而提前至 7 月支付，使單月支出受到付款時點影響。 收入方面，8
月關稅淨收入約 128.4 億美元，扭轉 7 月關稅淨收入為負 85.5 億美元的情況；累
計前 11 個月關稅淨收入約 1,673.1 億美元，較去年同期增加 1.3%。 支出方面，
8 月淨利息支出約 856.1 億美元，累計前 11 個月達約 1 兆 169.7 億美元，年增
8.9%，顯示利息負擔持續增加。 累計 2026 財年前 11 個月，美國聯邦政府財政
赤字約 1 兆 9,655.9 億美元，較去年同期小幅縮減 0.4%，其中收入年增 3.3%至約
4 兆 8,454.5 億美元，支出年增 2.2%至約 6 兆 8,110.4 億美元。整體而言，8 月單
月赤字雖明顯縮減 ， 但部分受到付款時點移轉影響 ， 且累計赤字仍接近2 兆美元 ，
加上利息支出持續攀升，美國聯邦財政壓力仍維持高檔。 
2026 年 8 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率降至 398 萬戶 ， 較上月減少2.0%，
較去年同期下降 1.2%，並為 2025 年 6 月以來首次跌破 400 萬戶，顯示高房貸利
率持續抑制房市交易動能。區域表現方面，西部銷售持平，東北部、中西部及南
部分別月減 4.0%、3.1%及 1.6%；房價方面，成屋售價中位數為42.91 萬美元，年
增 1.6%，連續 38 個月上漲。供給端則持續改善，待售成屋庫存增至 162 萬戶，
月增 3.2%、年增 5.9%，相當於 4.9 個月供給量，為近 10 多年來最高水準，住房
可負擔指數亦由去年同期的 101.2 升至 104.7。整體而言，8 月美國成屋市場呈現
銷售放緩、房價續漲及庫存增加的格局，高利率仍抑制成交動能，惟供給增加及
可負擔性改善，有助提升購屋者的選擇與議價空間，並促進市場逐步趨於平衡。 
2026 年 8 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.4%，高於上月的
0.1%，年增率維持 3.4%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，高於前月的 0.2%，但年增率由
2.5%降至 2.4%。在細項方面，能源價格由跌轉升，能源指數月增 2.1%，其中汽
油價格上漲 3.9%，成為推升整體物價的主要因素；住房指數月增 0.3%，食品指

數月增 0.1%。此外，通訊、外宿住宿、機票、教育及車輛價格均呈上漲，醫療照

2026 年 8 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 0.4%，高於上月之
0.1%，年增率則由上月的 4.8%升至 5.4%。其中最終需求商品價格月增 1.1%，結
束前兩個月下跌走勢 ， 超過四分之三的漲幅係由能源價格月增4.2%所帶動 ； 細項
中，柴油價格大漲 24.1%，汽油、航空燃油及燃料油價格亦同步上升，食品價格
則微增 0.1%。另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.1%，已連續第三個月上升，
主要受運輸及倉儲服務價格上漲 2.3%支撐 ， 其中貨車貨運服務及航空客運服務價
格分別上漲 2.0%及 4.2%，惟批發及零售業利潤率下滑，使部分服務價格漲幅受
到抵銷。此外，剔除食品、能源及貿易服務後之核心最終需求PPI 月增 0.3%，低
於 7 月之 0.4%，年增率維持 4.7%。整體而言，8 月 PPI 回升主要反映能源價格反
彈及運輸成本上升影響，顯示美國生產端通膨壓力仍具韌性；惟核心 PPI 增幅較
前月略為放緩，基礎通膨動能尚未進一步升溫，後續仍須持續關注能源價格波動
及服務成本變化對整體物價走勢之影響。 
近期美加貿易衝突持續升溫，雙方關稅報復行動已進一步擴大至非關稅措施
領域 。 加拿大自9 月 8 日起正式對約 200 億美元以上美國商品課徵 15%至 50%不
等的報復性關稅，涵蓋鋼鐵、鋁製品、家電、服飾及農業設備等產品，以回應美
國先前對加拿大商品加徵關稅措施 。 面對加拿大反制 ， 美國政府隨即宣布擴大限
制措施，除關稅措施外，將自 9 月 29 日起限制部分加拿大乳製品、酒類及機車
產品輸美，並限制加拿大企業參與部分美國政府採購標案，使雙方爭端由關稅戰
延伸至市場准入及政府採購領域。 加拿大總理 Mark Carney 表示，美國措施可
能衝擊加拿大關鍵產業發展，加方將持續採取對等回應措施。市場亦關注美方後
續可能進一步提高加拿大汽車、鋼鐵及相關零組件關稅，增添北美供應鏈及企業
投資規劃不確定性。整體而言，美加經貿關係短期內仍難見明顯緩和，後續發展
恐進一步增添北美及全球貿易前景不確定性。 
美國國會近期推進針對俄羅斯的新制裁法案，內容包括授權總統對俄羅斯原
油或天然氣主要購買國的進口商品課徵最高 100%關稅，以進一步限制俄羅斯能
源收入並加大對俄施壓 。 該法案已獲參議院以高票通過 ， 眾議院則預計展開審議 ，

二、歐元區—歐洲央行如期升息一碼，且仍有繼續升息的空間 
 歐元區第二季經濟成長季率終值由初值上修 0.2 個百分點至 0.6%，但上修主
因來自愛爾蘭經濟大幅成長 10.2%；若排除愛爾蘭，則歐元區第二季經濟成長季
率僅約 0.3%。從需求面觀察，第二季消費貢獻 0.2 個百分點，雖然通膨上升侵蝕
購買力，但薪資漲幅仍優於預期，加上高儲蓄率支持，使消費動能得以延續。其
中 ， 服務消費維持穩健成長 ， 耐久財支出亦明顯增加 。 另方面 ， 淨出口貢獻0.9 個
百分點，但設備投資下滑使整體固定投資表現仍顯疲弱。 
 歐洲央行 9 月 10 日如期宣布升息一碼，存款機制利率調升為 2.5%，但仍維
持「依經濟數據逐次決策」的立場。值得注意的是，本次歐洲央行分別上修2026
年、2027 年經濟成長率 0.1、0.2 個百分點至 0.9%、1.4%，以及 2028 年核心通膨
上修 0.1 個百分點至 2.3%， 反映歐洲央行認為經濟尚具韌性 ， 即便通膨壓力增溫 ，
仍能減輕其負面影響，使得政策利率繼續上調的空間，故一般預期歐洲央行將會
在 10 月政策會議採取觀望，並在12 月宣布升息一碼，2027 年第一季升息機率亦
有所上升，歐洲央行貨幣政策短期內仍將維持偏鷹立場。 
三、日本—內閣府上修第二季經濟成長，薪資動能續強 
2026 年第二季經濟成長率由 1.1%上修至 1.4%，主要受國內需求及庫存
貢獻改善帶動，其中以資本支出修正為國內需求上修的主因。展望下半年，
在政府財政刺激措施、企業持續擴大設備投資及薪資成長帶動消費支出的支
撐下，日本經濟可望維持溫和成長態勢，惟外部需求復甦力道、能源價格變
動及地緣政治風險仍為影響經濟表現的不確定因素。 
7 月薪資年增 4.7%，優於市場預期，其中夏季獎金年增 6.3%為主要推升
因素；經常性薪資及兼職時薪分別年增 4.1%及 5.7%，增速均較前期擴大 ， 顯
示薪資成長動能持續增強。此外，實質薪資年增3.4%，反映勞工購買力進一
步改善。在企業獲利表現穩健支撐下，儘管仍面臨成本上升及外部環境不確
定性 ， 企業仍維持獎金發放與加薪趨勢 ， 有助支撐民間消費韌性及經濟表現 。

四、中國—進出口同步強勁成長，貿易順差增幅則相對有限 
8 月出口年增率較 7 月回升 1.1 個百分點至 25%，出口總值為 4,014 億美元；
累計前八個月出口年增率為 19.3%，累積出口總值為 29,238 億美元。前八個月
IC、自動數據處理設備(包含伺服器等)累積出口額分別成長 103.9%、49.4%；船
舶累積出口成長 35.2%；汽車累積出口成長 53.2%。另方面，8 月進口年增率較 7
月回升 0.7 個百分點至 28.2%， 進口總值為2,824 億美元 ； 累計前八個月進口值成
長 28.2%至 21,183 億美元，其中 IC、自動數據處理設備(包含伺服器等)進口分別
成長 61.7%、105.1%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部分伺服器關鍵組件有賴
於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前八個月貿易順差達 8,055 億美元，僅較去年
同期增加 2.6%， 增幅明顯低於出口 ， 顯示出口擴張同時伴隨關鍵原物料進口大幅
增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價值產品依賴國際供應鏈支
持。 
8 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 7 月的 3.5%回升至 3.8%，主因原油價格自 7 月
起走升，其影響逐漸反映於上游生產成本。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格年
增率反彈 7.3 個百分點至 10.5%，並帶動燃料加工業通膨上揚，抵銷近期鋼鐵價
格下滑，導致相關礦產開採及冶煉加工產品價格較去年同期下滑的影響。此外，
對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學工業、化纖產業通膨沒有明顯變動，部分可能
與先前油價上漲期間累積較高庫存有關，惟在近期油價再度走升下，不排除相關
成本壓力於未來數月逐步反映。另因金價盤整，有色金屬礦採選業出廠價格年增
率亦持續放慢。 
8 月消費者通膨(CPI)回升 0.3 個百分點為 0.8%，但仍然是近七個月的次低水
準，核心通膨(不含食品及能源)回升 0.1 個百分點至 1 %。當月受國際原油價格上
漲影響，燃油通膨反彈 7.5 個百分點至 8.3%；手機零組件上漲，通信工具通膨上
揚 2.3 個百分點至 10.6%；夏季旅遊需求支持旅遊服務通膨增加 0.1 個百分點至
2.7%；其他商品及服務價格則大致平穩。整體而言，8 月能源價格有所回升，但
在企業庫存水位仍偏高情況下，抑制成本的傳導速度，除燃油價格外，尚未明顯
反映於其他消費項目通膨。惟考量能源成本傳導存在時間差，若油價維持高檔或
進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。

用相關內容。

8

五、印度及東南亞—油價上揚、通膨升溫，進口成本上漲恐使貿易收支惡化 
東南亞:(1)印尼：7 月出口額 262.2 億美元、年增 6.05%，非油氣產品出口年增
6.84%，礦物燃料、鋼鐵及電氣機械及設備出口增幅均逾 2 成，油氣產品出口
大幅下滑 14.58%；對美國（8.61%）、中國（17.52%）、日本（7.93%）、東
協（16.72%） 出口強勁成長。7 月進口年增 27.02%達 260.9 億美元，創歷史新
高，非油氣產品及油氣產品進口分別年增 23.83%、49.91%，以機械設備、電
機及電氣設備及塑膠品進口成長為主 ， 自中國、 歐盟進口成長23.49%、26.32%、
自美進口成長 13.1%。進出口相抵，7 月貿易收支意外轉為順差 1.3 億美元。
印尼經濟較依賴進口 ， 部分進口需求係配合國內投資及生產活動擴張 ，另國際
油價上漲增加能源進口負擔，在出口成長幅度相對有限情形下 ， 進口持續快速
增加將壓縮商品貿易順差，並可能進一步擴大經常帳赤字。8 月消費者物價指
數(CPI)年增率升至 3.19%，主要因食品、飲料和香菸價格上漲，以及個人照護
和交通成本增加所致 ，剔除波動較大的食品和政府管制價格的核心通膨率升至
2.92%，為 2023 年 3 月以來最高。儘管通膨有所回升，但仍維持在印尼央行
1.5%至 3.5%的目標區間內。(2)泰國：8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率上升至
2.53%，主要因中東衝突導致油價上漲，進而推高國內燃料價格所致，食品、
非食品價格通膨分別上升 2.99%、2.23%，剔除能源及生鮮食品的核心通膨率
上揚至 1.44%，創 3 年多新高，泰國商務部維持其 2026 年整體通膨預期在
1.5%-2.5%之間，通膨水準仍處於泰國央行 1%-3%的目標區間內。(3)菲律賓：
8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率從 7 月的 6.2%降至 6.1%， 主要因食品/非酒精
飲料、房屋/公用事業價格漲幅放緩，惟受燃油價格上漲影響，交通運輸價格
進一步上揚 13.5%，核心通膨率小幅降至 4.1%，1 月至 8 月平均通膨率為 5.2%，
仍高於菲律賓央行 2%-4%的目標區間，在國際油價大幅上揚下，央行升息壓
力漸增。(4)越南: 8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率上升至 4.89%，創 3 個月新
高，主要是運輸成本上升所致，儘管高於越南國家銀行(SBV)4.5%目標，惟核
心通膨穩定於 4.5%， 在經濟動能仍佳下 ， 央行尚有餘裕採取審慎觀望的政策。
8 月出口額年增 26%至 548 億美元，其中電子產品出口成長 41%為主要驅動
力，機械設備成長 34%居次，對美國出口大增 24.7%；8 月進口額年增 37.9%
至 549.1 億美元，以電子產品、電腦及零件成長 80%為最，機械設備居次。進
出口相抵，8 月貿易逆差收窄至 11 億美元。整體而言，越南受惠於人工智慧
需求及中國供應鏈移轉，帶動電子產品出口，但主要集中於外資企業，資本財

與生產投入高度仰賴進口， 對國內生產活動增值十分有限 ，越南政府正積極推
動投資技術轉移、提升治理能力及開拓市場，加強本土企業之間的聯繫，以創
造更大的附加價值。 
五、台灣—通膨略降溫，AI 需求支撐出口成長 
8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 2.04%，雖較前兩月回落，但已連續 4 個月高
於 2%的通膨警戒線，主要受國際油價上漲帶動油料費、機票及旅遊團費增加，
加上外食費、房租及電費等價格上漲所致；惟蔬菜價格因去年基期較高而大幅下
跌，抵銷部分漲幅。整體而言，通膨壓力雖略有降溫，但核心 CPI 仍達 2.30%，
顯示外食、房租等服務類價格仍具黏著性，物價上漲壓力尚未完全消退。 
 8 月出口達 824 億美元，年增 41%，續創歷年單月新高，主要受惠於人工智
慧應用快速擴展及國際雲端服務業者持續加大資本支出，帶動半導體、伺服器及
相關資通訊產品需求強勁。雖然部分傳統產業仍受全球景氣復甦步調不一及貿易
環境不確定性影響，但科技產品需求熱絡使整體出口表現優於預期。展望未來，
在 AI、 高效能運算及雲端服務需求持續成長 ， 以及第四季傳統外銷旺季來臨帶動
下，我國出口動能可望延續，惟仍須關注地緣政治風險及國際貿易政策變化對外
需之影響。

上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 聯準會上周三決議升息一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，FOMC 政策聲明顯示，18 名
FOMC 成員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次 ， 另有4 人認為存在升息兩次的可
能性 ，加上聯準會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)會後表達控制通膨的偏鷹立場 ， 帶動美
元指數走升至 100 高位。
⚫ 歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數
據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解 、 能源價格持續高位情況下 ， 市場對於歐元區經
濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度。
⚫ 美國公布初請失業金人數為 19.6 萬人，優於市場預期的 20.8 萬，再度呼應聯準
會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)認為目前勞動力市場仍具韌性的說法， 在美國經濟穩
健及通膨頑固的情況下，預計聯準會將持續升息，有利美元持穩。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委暨葡萄牙央行總裁佩雷拉(Alvaro Santos Pereira)表示，歐元區目前

通膨雖未廣泛擴散 ， 惟考量近期天然氣價格飆升 ， 央行將密切關注未來通膨壓力
擴散情形。最新利率期貨市場顯示，歐洲央行今年底仍有再升息一碼空間。
⚫ 日本政府通過消費稅減稅及家庭現金補貼的政策大綱，預計自明年 4 月起的兩
年內，將食品消費稅率自 8%降至 1%，惟大綱中未明確說明資金來源，再度引
發市場對於日本政府的財政擔憂，短線不利日圓表現。
⚫ 台灣央行 9/17 宣布重貼現率連 10 凍維持在 2%不變，央行總裁楊金龍表示，台
灣的通膨相較其他主要經濟體溫和，採取漸進緊縮貨幣政策有助物價穩定。 此
外，目前通膨率為 2%，明年有望降至 2%以下，後續將觀察通膨發展、主要經
濟體貨幣政策緊縮程度及台灣金融情勢，以決定未來貨幣政策走向。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.650，低點在 31.958，以 31.808 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶 ， 美國CPI 數據公布後加強市場對於聯準會 9 月升息的預期 ，
早盤台幣跳空開低，外資連續三日賣超台股並匯出，加以出口商態度轉為惜售，令
台幣接連貶破 31.700、31.800、31.900 關卡， 週四由於聯準會偏鷹派升息，美元指
數走升至 100.300，早盤台幣跳空開低，台幣最低貶至31.958，不過隨後出口商賣壓
出籠，外資雙向進出，台幣收復部分跌幅，維持在31.900 附近震盪；臨近周末，外
資買超台股並於外匯市場匯入 ， 台幣走升至31.722， 最終在央行調節之下收在31.808。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場而言 ， 美國最新初領失業救濟金人數較預
期減少一萬多人、續領失業救濟金人數亦下降，顯示美國勞動力市場尚屬穩健，加
以聯準會升息抗通膨，鷹派立場給予美元指數支撐、不利台幣表現；就台灣市場來
看，九月台灣央行並未升息，台美利差擴大預計將帶給台幣壓力，此外，儘管聯準
會升息利空出盡，外資買超台股帶動台幣走升，惟台灣即將迎來中秋連假，預計金
融市場交投謹慎，將令台幣陷入橫盤整理格局，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間
震盪。

卡，顯示 USD/CNH 空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪走升。

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走貶。 週初，美國通膨數據維持高位 ，令聯準會升息預期升溫 ，USD/JPY
自 153.29 低點反彈至 155 附近；週間 ，美國聯準會宣布升息一碼 ， 帶動USD/JPY 上
漲至 156.41；臨近週末，日本央行如市場預期升息一碼，惟央行偏鴿派立場令日圓
續貶，帶動 USD/JPY 一度最高來到 158.05，而隨後在日本政府於外匯市場進行匯率
詢價下，USD/JPY 回吐部分漲幅，終場收在 156.86，週線上漲 349 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本政府上周通過消費稅減
稅及家庭現金補貼政策大綱，惟大綱中未明確說明財源，再度引發市場對於日本政
府財政惡化的擔憂，預計將不利日圓走勢。二、日本央行上周五決議將政策利率調
升一碼至 1.25%， 9 名決策委員中有 2 人對升息持反對意見，降低市場對日本央行
進一步升息的預期。另一方面 ， 聯準會上周三亦升息一碼至3.75%-4.00%區間 ，FOMC
政策聲明顯示，18 位官員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次，另有 4 人認為存在升
息兩次的可能性，聯準會升息立場明顯較日本央行更為鷹派，預計將支撐USD/JPY
走勢。三、日本財務大臣片山皋月近期再度重申，將於必要時採取外匯干預行動，
以維護外匯市場有序運行。四、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 短中天期均
線向下發散走勢偏空，惟近期匯價在多次向下測試 154 均拉回，明顯下方有買盤支
撐。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：154.80-159.50

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，聯準會升息預期升溫加上歐元區 9 月經濟景氣指
數下滑，令歐元走跌至 1.1525；週間，美國聯準會鷹派升息立場帶動美元走升，歐
元最低來到 1.1460；臨近週末，歐元持續偏弱整理，終場收在 1.1483，週線下跌 107
點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、 歐洲央行管委卡薩克斯
(Martins Kazaks) 表示 ， 為避免美伊戰爭引發的燃料成本上漲開始傳導至工資和其他
商品價格，歐洲央行可能需要漸進式調升利率以抑制通膨。最新利率期貨市場顯示
歐洲央行今年仍有升息一碼的空間，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國聯準會決議將
利率調升一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，聯準會主席華許會後偏鷹言論帶動美元指數走
升至 100 上方，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自
前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解、能源
價格持續高位情況下，市場對於歐元區經濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度，將不利歐元
表現。四、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 近期回測年線未能有效突破後明顯
拉回轉為下行走勢，目前匯價承壓於各天期均線下方，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週央行理監事會議決議結果 ， 維持政策利率不變 ， 符合
市場預期 ， 且近兩週央行持續減發定存單 ， 令市場短錢充裕 ， 惟週五銀行間分
配略為不均，部分銀行有資金需求，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.70%，利率高位較上週上升 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投清淡，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三等殖系統成交在 1.86%，10 年
期指標券 A15106 則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.92~1.921%。 
台灣央行理監事會議如市場預期維持政策利率不變 ， 總裁楊金龍表示 ， 今年國
內通膨仍屬可控，明年有望回落至 2%以下，市場對今年升息疑慮有所降溫，
對台債形成支撐 ， 惟國際債市走勢仍偏空 ， 預期台債殖利率短線仍呈高檔區間
盤整格局，後續關注下週三財政部公布的第四季公債發行計劃，預估 5 年券
區間為 1.79%~1.83%，10 年券區間為 1.90%~1.95%。

長榮 (2603 TT) 
2026 獲利亮眼且擁高現金殖利率

永豐觀點 
2026 年獲利逆勢上揚且淡季運價緩跌有撐，擁8%現金殖利率。觀察12 月巴拿馬
運河水位。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：(1) 美洲持續暢旺到明年春節，亞洲進入旺季，運價淡季相對有撐，(2) 
2027 年新增運力被碼頭作業能量不易擴充而抵銷，實質有效運力可能低於市場預
期，(3) 高現金殖利率的保護，下檔有撐，維持買進，目標價由 256 元(0.9X2026 
BVPS)上調到 278 元(0.85X2027BVPS)。

ESG 評析 
長榮企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

長榮為全球第 7 大海運業者：長榮海運是全球第 7 大航商，6 月底集團運力 204
萬 TEU，242 艘船舶，市佔率 5.9%，2025 年底運力 195.6 萬 TEU(+11%YoY)，
2026-2027 運力分別為 207.1 萬 TEU(+5.9%YoY)和 241 萬 TEU(+15.9%YoY)，
待交船 72 艘約 93.5 萬 TEU。1H26 年運送 545 萬 TEU(+4.2%YoY)，平均運費
1034 美元/TEU(-7%YoY)，2025 年航線別美洲線佔 39%，歐洲線佔 28%、亞洲
線佔 18%，其他佔 15%。 
3Q 運價強勢 ，4Q 運價相對有撐 ：1H26 美洲線艙位成長 0.96%， 貨量成長5.1%，
歐洲線艙位成長 7.5%，貨量成長 12.2%。地緣政治風險導致供應鏈不穩旺季提前
運送而上漲， 亞洲重要港口碼頭使用率偏高， 天候因素容易導致碼頭作業效率低造
成塞港與巴拿馬運河水位降低等均影響有效運力，支撐美東線運價重回 1 萬美元
/FEU 關卡，10/8 巴拿馬運河水位 85.2 英尺(-1.93%YoY)較 9 月中略有回升，但
11 月底水位雖回升到 85.9 英尺但低於去年同期 3.4%，航商預期美洲線貨量持續
暢旺到農曆春節，觀察 2Q27 美國是否啟動升息循環影響貨量。

2026 年供過增長率最低的一年：Alphaliner 預估 2026 需求成長 2.5%， 供給成長
率 4.7%，2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的，目前貨櫃船在手訂單1400 萬
TEU，未來三年面臨新船大量下水壓力，然而公司認為受到地緣政治影響船期不穩
定性高、碼頭使用率偏高易受突發事件導致塞港、2028 年美西港口工人合約到期
等均影響有效運力，即使紅海復航採逐班逐航線，影響也會被抵銷，因此2027 年
的名目供給能否 100%轉為實質運力有待考驗 。 目前全球運力因為碼頭作業能量受
限、氣候因素、繞道、降速等影響 11%運力。 
美國零售進口 2026 年預計成長 1%：根據美國零售協會預估，2026 年 1-7 月貨
櫃進口 14.97 百萬 TEU(+0.3%YoY) ， 9 月進口量達到高峰 2.31 百萬
TEU(+10%YoY)，10 月開始下滑，但仍保持年成長，2026 年預估進口 25.7 百萬
TEU(+1%YoY)。

燃油成本的影響 ：新加坡船用燃油 3Q 月平均 574 美元/噸(-7%QoQ%+44%YoY)，
低硫油價格 3Q 月平均 798 美元/噸(+3%QoQ%,+58%YoY)，長榮安裝脫硫塔的
船舶超過 9 成，船齡相對年輕省油，2025 年燃油成本佔 21%，1H26 佔 22%，
2Q 反應油價上漲會提升。長榮 2025 年平均用油價格 485 美元/噸(-8.1%YoY)，
1-8M26 用油價格 581 美元/噸(+16.7%YoY)，1-8 月燃油成本提升到 23%。 
現金殖利率 ：預估明後年配發現金股利 20 元和 18 元 ， 現金殖利率為8.6%和 7.7%，
長線受高股息 ETF 的青睞。 
預估 3Q26 稅後獲利 413 億元(+90%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 19.08 元：3Q 旺季運價
亮眼 ， 營收1468 億元(+51%YoY)符合預期 ， 預估稅後獲利413 億元(+90%YoY)，

1320 億元(+54%YoY)，稅後獲利 264 億元(+210%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 12.20 元。

## Document 115: 261015_kgi_TSMC

### Page 1

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 IC製造 ‧ 台灣

台積電 
(2330.TW /2330 TT)

多、更多、再更多的 N3 產能擴張 
重要訊息 
台積電 3Q26 財報表現符合預期，管理層預估 2026 年營收將年增 44%。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 符合預期，4Q26 展望強勁。3Q26 毛利率 66.4%，受 N2 量產爬坡稀
釋影響，較我們預估低 1.6 個百分點，但符合公司財測。受惠營收成長及營
業費用控管，營業利益及營益率均符合預期。台積電 4Q26 營收財測為 523
億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增 14%，較我們先前預估高出 7%，毛
利率及營益率則預期持平，主要受惠 HPC 需求強勁及 N2 量產爬坡。 
N3 持續擴產，並評估美國亞利桑那州以外的擴產機會 。隨 4Q26 展望轉
強，台積電將 2026 年美元營收成長預估由原先 40%以上上調至 44%，並
維持全年資本支出 600 億至 640 億美元。台積電過去鮮少將單一製程產能
擴充至超過原先規劃的目標( 約每月 15 萬片)。然而，除近期宣布的三座晶圓
廠(台南 Fab 18 、亞利桑那州二廠及熊本二廠) 外，台積電再宣布於台南新增
一座 N3 晶圓廠，預計於 1H28 量產。整體而言，我們預估 N3 產能將由
2026 年底的每月 15 萬至 16 萬片，在未來兩年進一步增加 8 萬片，增幅達
50%。我們認為此次擴產反映三項需求：(1)Agentic AI 帶動的 CPU 需求；
(2)HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒；以及(3)AI ASIC 。此外，台積電亦正評估在美國
亞利桑那州以外地區擴產的可能性，例如德州，但目前尚未做出最終決定。  
不會將任何商機拱手讓給競爭對手 。管理層多次強調不會讓競爭對手取得任
何商機，體現在 N3 產能的積極擴充。此外，儘管我們預期 Google(美 )委託
Intel(美 )生產的 Humufish 目前沒有備援計劃。然而，在必要情況下，台積電
將協助客戶確保產品如期交付， 暗示若 Intel 的 EMIB-T 製程進度落後，台積
電有望進一步提升市占率。部分投資人可能擔憂台積電導入 ASML High -NA
設備的時程較競爭對手晚兩年，進而影響其技術領先地位。但我們持不同看
法，採用最新設備並不必然代表技術領先。若 在 2030 年前台積電能在不投
資昂貴設備的情況下達到相近的製程效能，反而 有望取得相較同業更顯著的
成本優勢。 
投資建議  
儘管 3Q26 財報符合預期，且我們預估 4Q26 表現將更為強勁，但在市場預
期極高下，投資人仍可能對短期毛利率走勢有所疑慮。考量未來的成長動能
及穩健的執行能力，我們建議投資人趁股價回檔時逢低布局。我們僅微幅調
整獲利預估， 但隨評價基準遞延至 2028 年，我們 將目標價上調至 3,650
元，仍基於景氣上行週期平均的 20 倍本益比，維持「增加持股」評等 。 
投資風險  
全球景氣不確定性；積極擴產導致供給過剩。  
增加持股‧維持

### Page 2

台灣 台積電

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

法說會重點數據 
 4Q26 財測： 營收預估為 523 億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增
14%；毛利率 65-67%，營益率 56.5- 58.5%。 
 2026 年展望：全年營收成長預估上調至年增 44%，維持資本支出 600
億至 640 億美元。  
 毛利率稀釋：海外擴產預計稀釋 2026 年毛利率 2 個百分點，未來幾年
稀釋幅度將擴大至 3-4 個百分點。  
 產能規劃：  
 N2 製程家族產能預計於 2027 年增加至兩倍以上。  
 台南新增 N3 晶圓廠預計於 1H27 量產，另一座 N3 晶圓廠則規劃於
1H28 量產。 
 亞利桑那州 N3 二廠已完成廠房建設，目前正進行設備搬入及安
裝，預計於 3Q27 量產，略早於原訂時程。  
 日本 N3 製程預計於 2028 年量產。 
 A14 製程已開始進行產能準備，預計於 2027 年如期試產，並於
2028 年量產。

圖 5：公司概況  圖 6：台積電各製程晶圓營收比重  
台積電是全球最大的專業半 導體晶圓代工廠， 近年來其市占率
持續有所增加。 2013 年的八吋約當產能超越一千六百萬片。
台積電現有六座先進十二吋晶圓廠、六座八吋廠、一座六吋
廠，外加正在興建的一座十二吋廠。台積電提供以下服務：晶
圓製造，晶圓探測，組裝和測試，光罩生產和設計服務。

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 6

ESG 圖表定義  
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力(公用事業為出售而生產) 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者(太陽能、風能、水能、生
物質能、地熱能)之量 
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2約當排放量 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

CO2約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸)銷售(百萬元)比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因(自願或非自願)離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 = (離開之員工/平均員工人數)*100 
- 平均員工人數 = (本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數)/2 
- 本財年末員工數 = 上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練(如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展) 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1天 = 8小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv，凱基

## Document 119: 261015_ms_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261015_ms_TSMC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
TSMC  | Asia Pacific
No slowdown of AI semi
demand; reiterate OW
TSMC is further expanding capacity after considering other
constraints, such as data center build, power and other
components supply.  We think the risk of over-capacity is small,
and stay OW.
TSMC sees robust AI semi demand: Despite CSP power shortages and rising
financing costs, TSMC sees demand far above its capacity. It sees no signs of a
slowdown, which may ease some market concerns. The company did not update its

AI semi CAGR guidance (55-60%), saying it is not meaningful as  the demand is
expanding rapidly.
Comments on  wafer foundry partnership (TeraFab) and competition (Intel): For
overseas fabs, TSMC is evaluating a TeraFab partnership, but TSMC must run the
partner fab. TSMC is also considering other fab campus locations, including Texas.
We asked management about IDM outsourcing from Intel and implications for A14
capacity demand. Management said it would try to be fair to all customers. On

competition, TSMC would expand capacity and “leave no food on the table” for
competitors.
On advanced packaging, we asked whether TSMC would help customers using
Intel EMIB-T advanced packaging: The company is confident about 9x and 14x
reticle size chip support. TSMC’s principle is to help customers ramp products
smoothly, so we take it as no objection if Google/MediaTek needs to adopt CoWoS/
CoPoS in future TPU versions, as we articulated in MediaTek: TSMC CoWoS support

eases MediaTek's 2028 TPU production concerns
We raise 2027/28 capex estimates to factor in 2nm and 1.4nm expansion: The
company maintained its 2026 capex guidance (US$60-64bn) and didn’t provide
next-year capex guidance. That said, it  mentioned more than a doubling of 2nm
capacity build by 2027.  Our initial cross checks with equipment venders suggest
EUV tool bookings double in 2027 (vs. 2026), followed by 25-30% growth in 2028.
We therefore raise our 2027/28 capex assumption to US$83/92bn.

3Q26 results review – margin miss, but revenue upside: Pease see Exhibit 1  and
Exhibit 2  for our earnings review. We lower our 2026 and 2027 margin
assumptions,  offset by higher revenue. We maintain our price target at NT$3,088
and reiterate our OW rating.

TSMC 3Q26 earnings and 4Q26 guidance  review
3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance review – margin miss, but revenue upside: 3Q26
margin of 66.4% misses our forecast of 67.5%, but was largely in-line with consensus,
given the margin dilution of 2nm node and overseas operation dilution.
The 4Q26 margin midpoint of 66% shows continued pressure. TSMC guides for 4Q26
revenue to grow 14% Q/Q at the midpoint on very strong demand, in line with our 10-15%

Q/Q expectation. Full-year revenue guidance is raised to 44% Y/Y, also similar to our
preview.

TSMC: Earnings estimate revisions
Our earnings forecasts decline 3% for 2026,  3% for  2027 and is largely unchanged  for
2028: We lower our 2026/27 earnings forecast mostly due to the margin miss of 3Q26
results and weaker than our initial expectation of 4Q26 margin guidance, and also the
wider overseas fab margin dilution into 2027. We slightly raise the revenue assumption for
2027/2028 as TSMC suggested it would speed up the 3nm/2nm capacity build, but that

also comes with larger depreciation and margin pressure when new production ramps.

Our PT is unchanged at NT$3,088. Our PT implies a 20x P/E multiple on our new 2027

EPS forecast. TSMC currently trades at 17x that estimate, close to its average NTM P/E of
16.5x since 2018. We find the stock attractive at current levels.
We continue to derive our 12-month price target (base-case scenario) using a residual
income valuation framework. Our key assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of
equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of 2.0%), an
intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 4%.

Our bull and bear case scenario values remain at NT$3,720 and NT$1,700, respectively.

NT$3,720.00
24x 2027e EPS
TSMC dominates foundry services: 1)
Breakthrough in EUV tech and materials
accelerates node migration to 3nm and
beyond. 2) Intel outsources its server CPU
production to TSMC sooner than expected.
3) Intel or Samsung exits leading-edge
foundry business. 4) New tech megatrends
such as 6G or AI drive global semi revenue

growth and increase demand for leading
edge nodes. 5) Cost per transistor decreases
further along with node migration.
NT$3,088.00
20x 2027e EPS
Leading position in leading-edge logic
foundry services with some gross margin
expansion: 1) Global semi revenue CAGR is
sustained in the high single digits in 2025-
28. 2) TSMC continues to lead in market
share in 16nm, 7nm, 5nm, 3nm, and 2nm. 3)
It continues to observe robust AI-related
demand. 4) Cost per transistor does not

decrease meaningfully in 3nm and 2nm,
while capex per k is more demanding than
for 7nm and 5nm.
NT$1,700.00
11x 2027e EPS
TSMC's dominance in foundry business
erodes: 1) Global ex-memory semi revenue
growth is weaker than expected in 2025-28.
2) Declining trailing-edge market share
causes revenue shortfall. 3) Intel's
contribution is weaker than expected. Intel
and/or Samsung successfully develop
leading-edge process technology. 4) Intel's
foundry business execution proves

successful and weighs on TSMC with order
competition. 5) Demand for 2nm is less than
expected given high transistor cost for
foundry customers.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 127: 261018_ms_OCP-takeaways

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
CPO/NPO and micro     VCSEL based   optical solutions are the
focus for networking, while HVDC takes center stage for power.
Higher bandwidth and better power efficiency: In the networking space, the
migration into higher bandwidth continues, as players like Accton and Celestica
showcased their 1.6T network switches. We think a bigger theme is the effort to
improve the power efficiency of current mainstream pluggable optical solutions.

First off, one well-discussed approach is the adoption CPO or NPO, and it seems the
latter is gaining more attention due to the technical challenges in the former. Spec
standardization, such as CPX connectors, could further accelerate the proliferation,
in our view. Secondly, copper could still help, as Credo has its 1.6T AEC cables for
inter-tray transmission while many other vendors (Amphenol, TE Connectivity,
Molex, etc.) have their 448G CPC solutions for in-tray transmission. Lastly, the micro

VCSEL based optical modules from Credo and ALAB represent another power
efficient solution (12W/end at 1,6T), which has reliability comparable to copper
(100M hours of MTBF/MTBI) for a transmission distance of 50-100m. We believe
this micro VCSEL based solution could potentially replace the AEC for shorter
distance transmission when copper hits the physical limit in the medium term and
be integrated with the CPO architecture in the longer term. In sum, we believe the

above-mentioned solutions are likely to co-exist for different use cases.
800VDC for power delivery taking the center stage: While the Vera Rubin NVL72
rack already has the in-rack power shelf reference design (110KW/shelf at 3+1
redundancy), Nvidia unveiled another reference design this time, using one 660KW
800VDC power rack to support two Vera Rubin NVL72 racks. Moreover, we also
saw a few ±400VDC reference designs as well. Our conversations with companies

on the exhibition floor indicate that the HVDC adoption should start from late
2026, but likely at a small scale extending into 2027. The next checkpoint will be
Vera Rubin Ultra racks, where increasing power consumption and more hardware
devices for inter-rack scale-up network might take up more pace on the rack, though
the liquid cooled power shelf  should ease the space budget a bit. We believe both
solutions are progressing and will closely monitor the dynamics.

Stock picks: We believe that Bizlink (3665.TW) should be a beneficiary of the
proliferation of the micro VCSEL based optical solution, as it is the assembly partner
for Credo and potentially others as well. Chroma (2360.TW) should see drivers
from power testing, CPU/GPU/TPU SL T/FT, and photonics testing (pluggable/
CPO)tools. Delta( (2308.TW) and Lite-On (2301.TW) should continue to grow with
the increasing power consumption of AI clusters, but more meaningful growth

CPX and NPX all marching forward
Though CPO remains as the holy grail in the space, its yield issue and lower serviceability
still present challenges to the wider adoption in the near term. We believe that the efforts
will continue, but the industry is simultaneously pursuing other more practical solutions

to sort out the power consumption issue of the existing pluggable transceivers,  including
NPO (near-packaged optics), CPC (co-packaged copper) and NPC (near-packaged copper)
Compared to CPO, NPO compromises on the power consumption, but in exchange it eases
the stringent requirement on alignment and serviceability. Given its better technology
readiness and more mature supply chain, we think it might see good traction in the
coming years.

VCSEL based optical solutions gaining more attention
In addition to CPO/NPO/CPC/NPC designs, we see the micro VCSEL based optical
solutions are gaining more attention too. The value proposition is to leverage the wide
and slow architecture for a relatively short transmission distance of 50-100m. It can

current generation can enable 32Gbps of bandwidth per lane and the 100-200Gbps of
bandwidth per lane is in the roadmap.

In sum, different solutions of data transmission are emerging, trying to improve the power

Power becoming another bottleneck in the AI deployment
HVDC adoption emerging from Vera Rubin
In the power space, HVDC has been one of the key focuses in the AI cluster, as the rapid
increase in AI rack power density is creating growing challenges for conventional power-
distribution architectures. As rack-level power consumption rising from tens of kilowatts
to several hundreds of KWs, electrical losses, cable size, thermal management
requirements, and overall system complexity increase significantly. Under traditional low-

voltage power architectures, delivering larger amounts of power requires proportionally
higher current, which increases resistive losses and places greater stress on cables,
connectors, busbars, and power-conversion equipment.
At the same time, every power-conversion stage introduces efficiency losses and
generates additional heat. As AI deployments scale into multi-megawatt and eventually
gigawatt-level campuses, even small efficiency improvements can translate into

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
meaningful reductions in operating costs, cooling requirements, and infrastructure
investment. Consequently, the industry is increasingly focused on power architectures
that can reduce conversion stages, lower current levels, and improve overall power-
delivery efficiency.
Against this backdrop, high-voltage direct current (HVDC) is emerging as one of the most
important architectural upgrades for next-generation AI infrastructure. By increasing

distribution voltage, HVDC allows the same amount of power to be delivered at a lower
current, reducing conduction losses and improving system efficiency. Higher-voltage
architectures also improve scalability for ultra-high-density rack deployments by
alleviating some of the physical and thermal constraints associated with conventional
power-distribution systems.
Exhibit 18: Better power efficiency for 800VDC architecture
Source: Delta
Exhibit 19: Better efficiency leads to cost savings

Source: Delta
In a joint keynote speech by Delta and Nvidia, the roadmap illustrates the industry's
gradual migration toward higher-voltage power architectures. Earlier AI server generations
primarily utilized conventional power-delivery schemes centered around rack-level 48V
distribution. As rack power requirements increased with Blackwell-generation systems,
industry participants began evaluating higher-voltage architectures capable of supporting

substantially greater power density. Looking ahead, the industry is increasingly converging
around 800VDC as a potential next-generation standard for large-scale AI deployments,
particularly as rack-level power consumption approaches several hundred kilowatts. The
transition reflects the need to improve power-distribution efficiency while supporting the
continued growth of AI computing infrastructure.
Though we also saw the 800VDC power rack reference design for the Vera Rubin rack in a

1-to-2 configuration (one 660KW power rack supporting two Vera Rubin racks), we think
the adoption of this solution might be relatively low, since the Vera Rubin rack also has a
reference design for the in-rack power shelf design

### Page 11

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 11
Exhibit 20: 800VDC roadmap
Source: Delta
Exhibit 21: 800VDC reference design for VR
Source: Delta
Liquid cooled power shelf on the horizon
The next key check point to watch should be the Vera Rubin Ultra generation, as the
power consumption per rack shall continue to increase and there might be more hardware
devices to enable the inter-rack scale up network, both could take up more space on the
IT rack. That said, the power shelf will shift from air cooling into liquid cooling, which

could narrow the power shelf height by 1U to free up some space as well. Our checks
indicate that both the in-rack power shelf and 800VDC power rack solutions are ongoing
now, and we believe that rack space, power redundancy and system reliability are all the
factors being considered. We'll closely monitor developments on this front.
Exhibit 22: Liquid cooled power shelf
Source: Megmeet
Exhibit 23: Liquid cooled power shelf in rack
Source: Megmeet

Solid state transformers on the Nvidia RVL for the first time
In the MGX ecosystem board, where Nvidia usually list the recommenced vendor lists and
product samples, we saw SST this time. Delta, Heron. DG Matrax, Sungrow, Solaredge,
Megmeet, Luxshare, etc.

### Page 12

M
Idea
12
Exhibit 24: SST vendors on the Nvidia MGX ecosystem board
Source: Morgan Stanley
Looking further ahead, Solid-State Transformers (SSTs) represent a potential next-
generation evolution of power infrastructure. Unlike traditional transformers that operate
primarily as passive devices, SSTs leverage power electronics to provide voltage
conversion, power conditioning, and bidirectional energy management within a single
platform. This enables greater controllability and flexibility while supporting increasingly

digitalized power networks.
Although SSTs remain at an early stage of commercialization and face challenges related
to economics, reliability, and large-scale deployment, the technology aligns well with long-
term data center trends. As AI campuses evolve into utility-scale power consumers
incorporating energy storage, distributed generation, and advanced power management,
SSTs could eventually become a key enabling technology.

### Page 13

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 13
Therefore, while HVDC is expected to drive the next phase of power architecture
upgrades, SSTs represent a longer-term technology roadmap that could further transform
how future data centers distribute and manage electricity.

2030, as the technology begins to replace a portion of the >$15bn conventional
transformer market, particularly in smaller-power applications. While SST penetration is
expected to remain relatively modest versus the overall transformer market, the
opportunity is significant for relevant players in the longer term.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Semiconductors
OCP Wrap-up: Rising Optics and
Al Infrastructure Alternatives
Our US trip highlighted ICA/IPO traction, complementary rack-
management silicon, hardware security and CXL-based memory
pooling, with potential storage server upside.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA

Ethan.Jia@morganstanley.com                                                +852 3963-2287
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
ICA/IPO: Gaining traction, but cost still a hurdle. A China CSP outlined optical
form factors from XPO/NPO to interposer optics (IPO) and CPO, including

integration of IPO with interposer copper attach (ICA). TE Connectivity showcased
224G/448G NPO/CPO sockets and ICA sockets with 0.4mm pitch and up to 10,000
pins, offering high-density connectivity, low crosstalk and improved reworkability.
We see rising traction for ICA/IPO, with potential mass production in 2027   (For
more, see our recent CPO report).
Marvell IMC: Complementary to AST2700, not a replacement. Marvell
demonstrated the OCTEON-based infrastructure management controller (IMC)

addresses higher-level Al rack/cluster telemetry and control; ASPEED's AST2700
BMC continues to handle node-level security and remote management. Given
distinct functions and software ecosystems, we do not view IMC as a direct
AST2700 competitor. We also don't see an AST2700 production issue, supporting
an on-track ramp. The key debate is actually market share in TPU/Axion.
Caliptra: Expanding hardware Root of Trust opportunities. The open Caliptra

framework defines silicon-level Root of trust (RoT) for data-center processors and
accelerators, supporting secure boot, device identity and attestation eMemory
(3529.TW) subsidiary PUFsecurity offers PUFrt, providing NeoPUF-derived device,
NeoFuse OTP secure storage and hardware entropy as physical security building
blocks for Caliptra implementations. Broader adoption could support incremental
PUF/security-IP licensing, although monetization depends on qualifications and

customer design wins. (For more, see our quantum security report).
Other: CXL fabric, cooling and compute ramps: The microchannel lid
demonstration underscored the importance of chip-level thermal design as rack
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

Key Takeaways
ICA/IPO traction: China CSP and TE demonstrations suggest growing adoption,
although cost could be higher
IMC versus BMC: Marvell IMC complements AST2700; we see no production
disruption.
Hardware security: Caliptra strengthens the case for eMemory PUF-based
security IP.
CXL and thermal: CXL supports memory pooling: Cooling and ASIC/CPU ramps

remain in focus. Muse is likely driving storage server demand.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O or Over) - The stock's total return is expected to exceed the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry
team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E or Equal) - The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or

industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR) - Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's
industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U or Under) - The stock's total return is expected to be below the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry

team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

研 究  報 告                
樺漢(6414.TT) 
產業：電子–電腦及週邊設備
3Q26 營收季增 3%，4Q26 營收預估與 3Q26 相當 
 投資評價與建議 
未來營收成⻑端視北美建廠需求與AI ， 毛利率提升主要仰賴整併Kontron的綜
效：樺漢計畫拿下3億美元之實體AI市佔，工控物聯網成⻑將由邊緣AI帶動，
北美建廠需求使智慧工廠持續成⻑，毛利率則藉由產品組合調整、新服務模式
的滲透率與整併Kontron的綜效以向上提升。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收季增3%(年增48%) ， 智慧工廠3Q26營收季減2%(年增84%) ：3Q26
營收496.4億元季增3%(年增48%)符合預估值(496.5億元)，工業物聯網事業
群、智慧軟體與方案事業群及智慧工廠與廠務事業群，分別季增4%、9%及-
2%(年增44%、-9%及84%)，智慧軟體與方案事業群4Q26為政府及企業單位
結帳高峰，4Q26營收比重可望高於3Q26，有助提升毛利率。 
4Q26營收預估與3Q26相當，在手訂單達2,550億元以上： 4Q26營收預估與
3Q26相當，成⻑驅動主要來⾃AI應用擴展 （由邊緣運算走向智慧平台）、半導
體擴產（先進製程/封裝/AI基建） 、智慧製造升級（⾃動化/機器人/數位轉型）
及全球在地化供應鏈布局；2,550億元以上的在手訂單能見度已延伸至 2027
年，其中工控物聯網受惠邊緣AI與交通/網絡設備強勁需求，智慧廠務工程持
續獲益於台灣及全球半導體大廠擴產與高科技廠務需求 ， 智慧軟體與方案則提
供高毛利且穩定的經常性收入。 
 投資風險 
整併效益不彰、實體AI需求不如預期。

3Q26營收季增3%(年增48%)，智慧工廠3Q26營收季減2%(年增84%)：3Q26營收496.4億元季增3%(年增48%)符合預
估值(496.5億元)，工業物聯網事業群、智慧軟體與方案事業群及智慧工廠與廠務事業群，分別季增4%、9%及-2%(年增
44%、-9%及84%)，智慧軟體與方案事業群4Q26為政府及企業單位結帳高峰，4Q26營收比重可望高於3Q26，有助提
升毛利率；面對記憶體與原物料價格波動，樺漢已導入動態內部報價機制，並採取「鎖量、鎖價」策略，同時整合採購
與研發以開發替代方案，確保成本可控並維持毛利水準。 
樺漢三大事業群

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料，玉山投顧整理、繪製

3 
且穩定的經常性收入。 
樺漢看好實體AI：過去幾年全球聚焦於生成式AI，讓AI進入大眾生活與文字對話，樺漢認為生成式AI僅是起點，下⼀波
產業革命的關鍵在於實體AI，將讓AI開始進入真實世界，具備『看得見、聽得懂、想得通、做得到』的感知、理解、決
策與⾃主行動能力；根據Next Move Strategy Consulting預估，全球實體AI市場規模將由2025年的58 億美元成⻑至
2035年的1,200 億美元(CAGR35%)，10年預估成⻑將超過20倍；目前樺漢已全球部署超過100萬套系統，包含
Casino/Gaming (7.5萬/21萬套)、Lottery (6萬套)、ATM (25萬套)及POS/Self-Checkout (35萬套)，實體節點分布極為
廣泛；樺漢已打造⼀套共通核⼼技術平台，涵蓋Compute Platform (SOM/Edge)、Sensor Suite (Camera/LiDAR/IMU)
及Drive & Actuator、Connectivity (5G/Wi-Fi/TSN)，同⼀套平台可靈活⽀援 AMR（⾃主 移動機器人）、Mobile 
Manipulator （移動操作機器人）、Humanoid （人形機器人） 及Industrial Robot （工業機器人），大幅降低研發成本，
加速產線規模化複製落地 ； 樺漢機器人團隊已完成核⼼技術平台整合 。 早期 AMR 與工業⾃動化機器人已在智慧工廠與
物流場域大量量產；針對具備感知能力的人形機器人 （Humanoid）與 Mobile Manipulator，公司透過 NVIDIA Isaac
數位雙生進行驗證，預計於2026年起陸續展開客戶PoC與場域導入，並配合 Kontron 歐美通路擴大推廣，目標在10年
內拿下3億美元之實體AI市佔。 
樺漢與Kontron的整合策略定位

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料 
Kontron的EBITA/營收比率將由2026年的14%成⻑⾄2030年的16%：樺漢與Kontron之結合定位為 『One Platform， 
Two Strengths』，Kontron提供高階研發、KontronOS資安軟體與歐美品牌優勢，樺漢則提供全球規模化製造、供應
鏈採購與ODM執行能力，整合效益預期涵蓋四大構面 : 1、技術與產品整合，將KontronOS與5G/NAD/資安技術導入
樺漢硬體平台，拓展Edge AI / Physical AI方案，2、全球市場整合，實現亞洲、歐洲、北美區域交叉銷售與品牌聯合開
發，3、供應鏈整合，結合鴻海全球採購規模進行零組件採購優化，改善缺料及逾期積壓訂單，4、全球製造整合，執行
在地化製造策略，結合歐洲（捷克/保加利亞/匈牙利）、亞洲樺漢製造與北美區域生產；Kontron的2030年營收目標將
由2026年的16億歐元成⻑至2030年的26億歐元，EBITDA獲利目標將由2026年的2.25億歐元成⻑至2030年的4. 2億歐
元，EBITA/營收比率則將由 2026年的14% 成⻑至2030年的16%，軟 體解決方案收入將由 2026年的5.5億歐元倍增至 
2030年的10億歐元，核⼼成⻑動能包括鐵路通訊、國防航太及5G專網與關鍵連通技術；樺漢對Kontron持股已提高至
49.4% ， 總投資金額約2.87億歐元 ， 整合效應目標為EPS年貢獻2027年起達5元(獲利貢獻預估將佔稅前淨利的16%)以上 。

| 樺漢的定位與優勢 
樺漢是鴻海艦隊的工業電腦旗艦：樺漢科技（6414）是鴻海集團旗下的重要工業電腦（IPC）與工業物聯網廠，是鴻海
艦隊的工業電腦旗艦。鴻海透過旗下投資公司共持有樺漢約24%的股權，為其最大股東與⺟集團，雙方在資源、供應鏈
及高科技AI智慧製造上緊密合作。樺漢的產品應用領域非常多元，包括智慧零售、智慧監控、智慧家居、資訊安全、交
通、製造、能源、醫療、建築、園區等，且透過整合硬體、軟體與人工智慧驅動服務積極推進ESaaS(樺漢解決方案即服
務)計畫，以滿足客戶多樣化需求。 
三大事業群架構分別為工業物聯網、智慧軟體與方案及智慧工廠與廠務：樺漢的三大事業群架構分別為工業物聯網事業
群、智慧軟體與方案事業群及智慧工廠與廠務事業群；工業物聯網事業群包括邊緣運算、網安通訊、工業與嵌入式系統
整合 。 智慧軟體與方案事業群著重高毛利軟硬雲整合 ， 涵蓋工業軟體 、 機器學習 、 數位雙生 、 雲地平台系統整合 ， Kontron 
在此板塊扮演關鍵的歐洲市場與軟體加值核⼼。智慧工廠與廠務事業群提供半導體設備及廠務系統、智能系統整合、高
科技材料與設備，由帆宣(6196)主導。 
併購帶來營收規模快速成⻑，三大事業群各有所⻑：樺漢透過併購壯大規模，營收由2015年的111億元僅短短十年就快
速10倍成⻑至2025年的1,423億元 ， 尤其入股Kontron(8/20後樺漢持股49.4%)與帆宣更是關鍵 。 樺漢看中Kontron的品
牌加持與歐洲的高市佔率，目前軌道交通產品Kontron已掌握歐洲逾50%市佔，並著手把相關方案導入亞太市場。樺漢
和Kontron在技術合作方面，建立共享平台，全面整合研發、製造、供應鏈、IT和人才等資源，協同設計產品，建構具
有全球競爭力的製造系統；產品整合則共享全球品牌資源，提升市場認知度與價值定位，並深入推廣產品線至亞太、歐
洲、北美等全球市場中⼼；市場聚焦方面，結合Kontron在基礎作業系統(Kontron OS)、物聯網軟體和5G網通方面的軟

5 
實力，以及樺漢在邊緣AI伺服器、機器人和強固型工業電腦方面的硬體優勢，共同打造實體AI技術平台。 
樺漢併購帶來營收規模快速成⻑

資料來源 : CMoney，玉山投顧整理、繪製 
實體 AI 帶來新⼀輪商機，樺漢拚五年內全球工業電腦第⼀名：實體 AI 的應用廣泛，如⾃動搬運叉車可應用於倉儲及
工廠內物料搬運，具備即時導航與避障，可提升效率並降低人力依賴。服務型機器人則可應用於清潔、導引及巡檢，
具有穩定感知與定位，可適用商辦、醫院、公共空間。樺漢認為，生成式 AI 只是開始。下⼀個十年真正改變世界的，
不只是 AI 會回答問題，而是 AI 開始進入真實世界。逐漸開始看得見、聽得懂、想得通、做得到。實體 AI 的定義應該
是讓 AI 具備感知、理解、決策與行動能力。樺漢預估 2035 年的實體 AI 市場規模可達 1,200 億美元，樺漢欲藉此商
機於五年內攻佔全球工業電腦第⼀名。樺漢目前已透過旗下全資⼦公司超恩，強化邊緣運算產品線，並結合 NVIDIA
的 Nemotron 模型，處理影像、聲音與文字等多模態資料。這類架構把資料分析留在終端端點執行，對應即時反應與
隱私要求較高的場域，也減少雲端端 AI 運算負擔。樺漢同步延伸 ESaaS(Ennoconn Solution as a Service，樺漢即服
務)服務內容，將硬體、軟體與人工智慧驅動服務整合到同⼀套方案中。樺漢 AI 聚焦在應用層與電力能源層，應用在智
慧製造、智慧城市、ESG 以及發展利基小語言模型。

持續由零售與金融智慧化需求推動營收增⻑。未來三年營收目標呈現年增 10％、15％、25％的階梯式成⻑。Kontron

| 工業電腦產業簡介 
工業電腦對環境的適應力高於個人電腦：1984年，IBM發表了5531型工業電腦，隨後於1985年推出基於Intel 80286處
理器的IBM7531，正式開啟工業電腦新紀元。工業電腦（IPC）發展至今已有40多年的歷史，從最初只負責簡單的流程
監測，進化到現今具備高效能算力的邊緣控制中⼼，其最大的特⾊在於使用「工業級用料」，能夠保障⻑達10至 15年
的使用需求，並且能克服各式嚴苛的環境挑戰，確保設備能持續穩定地運作。工業電腦最顯著的特性便是其針對惡劣環
境的耐用性，為了應對容易有大量粉塵的環境，多數的機型都採用「無風扇」和「⼀體成形」的設計，避免風扇故障引
起的熱當機或灰塵吸入造成的短路；此外，為因應戶外作業或極端氣候，大部分的工業電腦都會通過寬溫(例如：可運行
的溫度區間為 -40°C～85°C)與寬壓(例如：可輸入電壓區間為 9V～36V)測試，並搭載防塵、防水、防震、耐衝擊的機
制，確保設備在震動頻繁的環境下仍能穩定運作，工業電腦要具備嚴苛環境的適應力，高可靠度，擁有豐富的工業接口
及較⻑的生命週期管理。 
工業電腦與⼀般電腦的差異

資料來源 : Google、 玉山投顧整理、編製

### Page 7

7 
工作溫度 、 防護等級等為評定工業電腦優劣的重要指標 ， 以冷卻機制來說 ， 個人電腦依賴主動散熱 ， 灰塵與故障風險高 ，
工業電腦則採被動、密封設計，降低⻑期失效機率；擴充與客製化方面，工業電腦可高度客製，對應工業、醫療、車載
等垂直場域需求，而非僅僅只有通用規格；電源管理能力方面，工業電腦具備抗突波與點火控制，能在不穩定供電下持
續運作；至於作業系統穩定性方面，工業電腦多採用⻑期⽀援版本，可避免更新中斷營運。 
工業電腦優劣的重要指標

資料來源 : Google、 玉山投顧整理、編製  
工業電腦的應用場域多元化：隨著技術的演進，工業電腦的應用場景早已跨越工廠的圍牆，在各個專業領域的解決方案
成功落地，從車載應用、軌道交通、船舶海事、無人載具、人型機器人、工業機器人、工業⾃動化、5G通訊網路到邊境
安防，工業電腦的應用場域多元化，隨處可見。 
工業電腦的分類與應用場景

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理、編製 
⾃從工業電腦從工廠走進我們生活日常的POS、交通售票系統、醫療診斷到博弈娛樂等，它的重要性就日益加重，舉例
來說 ， 於智慧醫療其應用於影像診斷與手術輔助 ， 核⼼在於精準度與醫療級電氣安全 ， 錯誤的成本極高 ； 智慧交通方面 ，
車載與軌道監控需⻑時間運算與抗環境⼲擾，任何中斷都直接影響公共安全。 
全球工業電腦市場2035年將達104億美元，工業電腦台廠各有專精：根據研調機構構Research Nester統計與預估，全
球工業電腦市場規模將由2025年的61億美元，成⻑至2035年的104億美元，年均複合成⻑率為7.9%，台廠從上游半導
體至下游電⼦組裝產業供應鏈完整，具上下游垂直整合優勢且各有專精。

台廠具上下游垂直整合優勢

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理  
AI時代帶來邊緣運算需求 
AI時代到來，工業電腦將從硬體代工走向邊緣運算平台化，軟體的整合能力將決定產品毛利率的高低，工業電腦也從第
⼀代的控制功能跨步到第⼆代的連網功能，再進而邁向大步往第三代的人工智慧及時推理功能前進。未來，工業電腦已
不再只是單純的控制設備，而是逐步成為集資料蒐集、即時運算與通訊於⼀⾝的邊緣中樞；定位於邊緣運算讓工業電腦
不再只是資料收集設備，而是真正具備「看得見、聽得懂、想得出判斷」的能力，能在工廠端即時完成視覺檢測與設備
健康判讀；更關鍵的是，這種結合正在實際解決⻑期困擾製造業的問題，那就是「經驗無法複製」，透過 AI 學習資深
工匠的判斷邏輯，工業電腦得以把隱性知識轉為可執行的數位規則，即使在技術人力短缺的情況下，生產品質依然能被
穩定維持。 
工業電腦的世代演進

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理、編製 
⾃從NVIDIA執行⻑黃仁勳宣告 「每⼀家工業公司都將成為機器人公司」，並將實體AI定義為繼生成式AI之後的下⼀波科
技浪潮後，AI便不再只是雲端的虛擬智慧，而是能感知環境、理解物理規律、並在真實世界採取行動的能力，這句話所
指向的核⼼硬體，正是工業電腦。未來工業電腦由於內建NPU、GPU等AI加速模組，將可在本地端即時分析影像、震動
等高頻資料，避免等待回傳結果，具有即時推論的效能；另外，也具備低延遲響應且降低頻寬需求，再加上模型的持續
優化，讓工業電腦扮演邊緣AI推論平台，除了整合機器視覺系統的算力核⼼，同時也協作機器人與人形機器人的控制大
腦。邊緣運算正在讓工業電腦從「事後記錄者」轉變為「即時預警者」，工業電腦不再只是設備，而會成為對抗全球勞
動力短缺、突破職人經驗瓶頸的關鍵工具，推動智慧製造從「改善」走向真正的「質變」，預估全球邊緣運算的市場規
模於2033年將可突破千億美元，年均複合成⻑率將可達21%。

工業電腦(IPC)3.0於2026年後將進入主體AI時代

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料 
工業電腦歷經成⻑及轉型升級 ， IPC 1.0 (2011至2015年)是工業電腦時代 ， 主要定義讓設備具備運算能力 。 IPC 2.0 (2016
至2020年)是工業物聯網時代，主要是讓設備彼此連結。而IPC 3.0 (2020至2025年)為智慧平台時代，目地是讓數據產生
價值。2026年之後進入主體AI時代，將讓AI從數據分析走向實際執行，從協助決策走向⾃主行動。

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 261: Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-10-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-10-24).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-24
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:04 October 23 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor
Hauser's fireside chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a follow-up rate hike at the
November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook was "materially worse" than the RBA's

### Page 2

August forecasts. Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having "crystallised" ahead of the
September meeting – the Middle East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser noted the
Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in
August”. He then positioned AI and data centre developments among an "extraordinary sequence of
positive demand shocks around the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through into

CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy Governor noted that "inflation's been coming
in at or a little bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the labour market". He went on to
state that the staff are currently assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement on
capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other words, revise up their assumption for the
NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of market pricing for a follow-up November

hike, from just a 20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition today ahead of next week's
Q3 CPI. For that release, we are forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a 0.9 (3.5%yr)
increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3
as well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast. Provided inflation prints broadly in line
with our expectations, the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.

Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3 and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to
3.7%yr. Under the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely watched measures of core
inflation took a step higher to now be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our NZ
economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October
policy review, while retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.

Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised to the upside in Q3, GDP growth accelerating from
0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on track to
achieve their 4.5-5.0% full-year growth objective.
While annual retail sales growth improved in September, rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace
of growth is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very weak versus history. Consumer
spending continues to be constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to employment

prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five years into the property downturn, house prices continue
to decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep downturn, -20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in
high-tech investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst domestically oriented businesses
as well as property’s malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and 2028 as the

contribution from net exports moderates, broad-based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect
recent targeted support measures to be broadened and given much greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr in September, exceeding both market
expectations and the Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely driven by energy, fuel
prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services

inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an immediate concern inflation, the labour market
showing resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to 4.7% in August, suggests demand

risks for inflation will need close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is
released. For both events, the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The week after, the
US mid-term elections are due.

## Document 300: France - 2027 Budget_ Fiscal consolidation must now resume (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/France - 2027 Budget_ Fiscal consolidation must now resume (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

2027 Budget: Fiscal consolidation must now resume

The 2027 budget, adopted by the French Council of Ministers today, calls for a reduction in France’s fiscal 
deficit to 5% of GDP in 2027 (down from 5.4% in 2026). This is a first step toward s the multi-year fiscal 
consolidation needed to stabilise the public debt-to-GDP ratio. This ratio is expected to rise further in 2027 
and until the deficit is reduced to 3% of GDP. Furthermore, given the increase in some expenditures (+0.6 pp 
of GDP for debt service and defence spending), the projected fiscal effort amounts to 1 pp. Nearly one-third 
of this effort would come from higher compulsory levies, and two -thirds from curbing government and 
social spending.

2027 Draft finance law: A step in the right direction. The government is forecasting an improvement in the fiscal 
deficit from 5.4% of GDP in 2026 to 5% in 2027. This projection is based on a credible growth assumption of 1% 
(see our analysis of French growth drivers). To achieve this target, compulsory levies (CL) are expected to rise to 
44.2% of GDP, an increase of 0.3 pp (the same increase as in 2026, compared with +0.9 pp in 2025). The public 
spending-to-GDP ratio would decline by 0.2 pp to 56.9%. The effort required to reduce the deficit to 5% is greater 
than it appears. Indeed, some expenditures will increase (interest payments by 0.4 pp and defence spending by 
0.2 pp). Consequently, the fiscal effort for next year amounts not to 0.4 pp, but to 1 pp of GDP. One-third of this will 
be achieved through increased CL, and two-thirds through spending cuts. Social spending will contribute, notably 
through the cancellation of EUR 3 billion in social contribution reductions for low-wage earners, as well as efforts 
totaling EUR 6 billion each in the areas of pensions and healthcare. Social spending shows the largest deviation 
from pre-COVID levels, with an increase representing 1.2 pp of GDP, nearly half of the gap between the 2026 
budget balance and a 3% deficit. Government spending, excluding interest payments and defence, is expected to 
be frozen in nominal terms.

A compromise seems likely despite a tight schedule. The French National Assembly will start reviewing the 
budget on Tuesday 13 October. The government will need to ensure that a majority vote against the draft budget 
does not form. The current circumstances, including increasing sovereign bond yields and the upcoming 
presidential election, call for a consensus based on fiscal consolidation of the size proposed by the government. If 
Parliament does not pass a budget by the end of the year, the government will have to choose between 
implementing the government’s budget proposal by executive order ( only possible if there was no parliamentary 
vote) or through a special law (as in 2025 and 2026). A special law would enable tax collection , while the 
government and social security agencies could continue to issue debt. In this case, the 2026 budget would apply 
in 2027 (without indexation) until Parliament passes a budget in the first few weeks of the year. This must occur 
before the end of February (when Parliament’s session ends, earlier than usual due to the presidential election).

The one-year postponement of the 5% budget deficit target has a measurable impact. The gap between the 
budget adopted for 2026 (deficit of 5% of GDP) and the actual budget outcome (deficit of 5.4%) is due to 
unforeseeable circumstances (the conflict in Iran  and extreme drought). However, postponing the deficit target  
previously set for 2026 to 2027 comes at a cost: 1) the public debt-to-GDP ratio is expected to reach 121% in 2027 
according to our forecasts (1 pp above our scenario with a 5% target in 2026 and a deficit reduced to 4.5% in 2027), 
and 2) the (upcoming) postponement to 2032 of the 3% of GDP deficit target (a commitment that most presidential 
candidates plan to make) rather than in 2029 (the government had committed to this with the European Commission 
in 2024). In 2032, the public debt-to-GDP ratio would stabilise at 124% of GDP (instead of 121.5% if the deficit had 
been reduced to 5% of GDP as early as 2026 and to 3% of GDP by the end of the decade).

Another delay in fiscal consolidation would further erode France’s credibility. This new delay could stem 
either from a decision by the government formed in May 2027 to immediately implement part of its agenda, or from 
the enactment of a special law that would remain in effect until (just after) the likely legislative elections in June  
2027  or  for  the  entire  year.  If  the  government  emerging  from  the  elections  were  to  resume  fiscal  
consolidation only in 2028 (with a deficit of 5% of GDP that it projects to reduce to 3% by 2032), public debt would 
reach 126% of GDP by that date (a one-year delay in fiscal consolidation would result in an additional 2 pp of 
debt). This further delay could potentially undermine the credibility of the next government, given that budget 
targets were  not met in three out of four years between 2023 and 2026, a situation that would be costly amid a 
sharp rise in long-term interest rates. Furthermore, a higher deficit would increase the amount of debt to be issued 
within an already  tight schedule. The Agence France Trésor (AFT) has announced debt issuances totaling EUR 
340 billion in 2027 (an  increase  of  EUR  20  billion  compared  to  2026).  This  increase  is  due  to  the  maturing  
COVID  related  debt (beginning in 2027 and continuing largely through 2030), while the amount of debt to be 
issued to finance the deficit will remain stable compared to 2026. A decrease from this last driver would be needed 
in subsequent years so that  the AFT’s debt issuance can stabilise.

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of the Advanced Economies Team, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

We expect a 25bp hike on 28 October by the Reserve Bank of
New Zealand. Since the dovish September meeting, rising oil
prices, higher front-end rates globally and weakness in the NZ
dollar all point to risks of inﬂation overshooting 4.0% in 4Q. Oﬀ-
meeting commentary has supported market bets on another
hike, and we expect a hawkish-leaning statement
Francesco Pesole

In September, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised rates to
2.75% but pushed back against expectations for a large tightening
cycle. Its projections pointed to the next hike only in December or
1Q27 and a 3.25% peak in 2028-2029.
The picture has turned increasingly hawkish since then. We now
expect a hike on 28 October, in line with market pricing,
accompanied by a hawkish statement. We expect another hike in
either December or February, with a slight preference for February,

and see chances of another move later in 2027. Our terminal rate
estimate is 3.25-3.5%.
Markets input has been hawkish for the
RBNZ
Source: ING, Macrobond
A lot has changed since September
Third-quarter inﬂation slowed from 4.1% to 3.7%, below both the
RBNZ’s 3.9% estimate and our 3.8% forecast. Non-tradable CPI, a
measure of core inﬂation, came in at 3.3% versus the RBNZ’s 3.6%
estimate. In isolation, those numbers would have reinforced
September’s dovish message and argued for an October hold.

Other developments, however, have argued for earlier action:
1. Higher energy prices. The RBNZ’s September projections
assumed oil prices would gradually decline. Instead, they have
risen around 20% since the September meeting. That has
materially increased the chances of 4Q inﬂation exceeding

2. Weaker Kiwi dollar. The trade-weighted value of NZD tumbled
4% in September, and is at the lowest since 2009 (chart below).
The RBNZ has been one of the culprits behind a weak NZD, and
while it doesn’t normally react to FX moves, the depreciation is

3. Resilient growth. Second-quarter GDP surprised to the upside

leaving the RBNZ more room to ﬁght inﬂation with higher rates.

at 15bp, but so is the starting point for rates. The RBA raised
rates again to 4.6% in September and kept the door open for

The focus on 4Q inﬂation seemed to us to be a signal for markets

Hawkish hike to help NZD
We see little reason for the RBNZ to hike and pair the move with
dovish signals. Renewed focus on the currency means its
communication may be more market-aware than usual, while
resilient growth allows it to err on the hawkish side.
There are no new projections at this meeting, giving the RBNZ more

ﬂexibility to adjust its policy message through the statement
without sounding committal on the rate path. We think the
message will focus on a) keeping the door open to a December
hike; and b) signalling that the hiking cycle can be longer and faster
than previously estimated. We expect this to validate market
pricing for around 75bp of tightening by end-1Q27.
We saw good upside potential for NZD in our latest monthly FX
update, as the currency looked cheap by short-term valuation

metrics and there was room for further hawkish repricing in NZD
front-end rates. Our 0.570 one-month target has already been
reached, and we admit further NZD/USD gains depend more on
global/US developments than domestic factors. Still, the pair is
roughly 1% undervalued in the short term, and some emerging
USD idiosyncratic weakness around AI concerns, combined with a
hawkish RBNZ hike, can help build a stronger ﬂoor and leave
potential for further exploration.

## Document 393: US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

An update on AI and US equities

Both stocks and investor views relating to the AI trade have been exceptionally 
volatile during the last few months. Most of the volatility has been concentrated in the 
AI infrastructure complex, but the latest earnings season also drove sharp moves in the 
hyperscalers and among software stocks. 
Below we address three of the most common AI-related questions we have received 
from clients in recent weeks: 
1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
2. What revenues are required to generate a sufficient return on recent AI 
investments? 
3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing?

1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
The largest US hyperscalers are on track to spend $800 billion in capex in 2026 and 
consensus estimates imply they will spend $1.1 trillion in 2027. Consensus 2027 
capex estimates have been revised higher by $175 billion since the start of the Q2 
earnings season. Commentary from the hyperscalers during earnings season described a 
supply-constrained environment and large revenue backlogs, and these factors are 
driving the companies to tap both debt and equity markets to support additional 
investment spending. Based on consensus estimates, capex in 2027 is on track to reach a

### Page 3

larger share of GDP than any technological investment cycle since the railroad build-out 
in the late 1800’s. 
Our baseline forecast is that hyperscaler capex spending will increase substantially 
in 2027 but that the rate of both capex growth and upside surprises to consensus 
estimates will diminish relative to recent quarters. GS equity analysts estimate that 
hyperscaler capex will register $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028. Their 
estimates are $130 billion and $100 billion above the consensus estimates, respectively, 
but nonetheless imply a deceleration in capex growth from nearly 100% in 2026 to 54% 
in 2027 and 12% in 2028.

The need for external financing and potential supply bottlenecks will likely 
constrain the rate of capex growth next year. Hyperscaler capex spending now 
exceeds cash flow from operations, which means large further growth will increasingly 
need funding via debt and equity issuance. While hyperscalers have added leverage, 
their balance sheets do not appear to be a binding constraint and their interest coverage 
ratios remain extremely healthy. Our credit strategists instead point to concentration in 
the investment grade market as the more relevant bottleneck, which will require 
additional funding from private credit and equity issuance. Potential government 
restrictions on data center construction or an intentional slowdown in frontier model 
roll-out could also act as potential constraints on spending, as could shortages of labor, 
power, memory, or other inputs. Constraints on capex represent a risk to the near-term 
earnings of AI infrastructure beneficiaries, but could help ease investor concerns about 
overbuilding.

Going forward, token demand growth will need to outpace declining token prices to 
support continued growth in investment spending. Frontier models are currently a 
key source of demand for hyperscaler compute. However, the rise of competitive 
open-source models has contributed to a decline in average token prices. Measures of 
frontier token demand slowed in July, simultaneous with sell-offs of many AI 
infrastructure stocks, but accelerated again in August. 
For most AI infrastructure companies, decelerating AI investment spending 
suggests decelerating EPS growth. AI infrastructure companies, and semis in 
particular, have been the clearest beneficiaries of the capex surge. Continued upward 
revisions to consensus 2027 capex estimates should support continued positive 
revisions to consensus 2027 EPS estimates for many AI infrastructure stocks, but the 
magnitude of revisions will likely diminish relative to what occurred in H1 2026.

2. What revenues are required to generate a return on AI investments? 
Our equity analysts estimate that the hyperscalers would need to generate annual 
AI revenues of roughly $300 billion in the next few years to break even on their 
average annual capex investments in 2026 and 2027. These estimates reflect their 
expectations for average annual AI capex in 2026 and 2027 for the US hyperscalers, 
including AMZN, GOOGL, META, MSFT, ORCL, and SPCX. The breakeven revenues 
required for this capex represent the sum of annual depreciation and operating 
expenses. Exhibit 8 provides an estimated sensitivity of required AI revenues the 
hyperscalers must generate in different return on invested capital (“ROIC”) scenarios. 
These required revenue estimates are anchored on average annual capex in 2026 and 
2027 and would argue for even larger estimates based on cumulative capex.

The hyperscalers’ current estimated AI revenues remain below the amount that 
would allow them to break even on their capex, but those revenues are growing 
quickly and revenue backlogs are sizable. The hyperscalers have started to monetize 
their AI investments. Year/year cloud revenue growth for AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT, and 
ORCL has accelerated from 25% in 2024 to 48% in Q2 2026. In dollar terms, annualized 
cloud revenues in excess of their 2024 growth rate registered nearly $70 billion in Q2 
and have been roughly doubling during the past few quarters. The three major US public 
cloud companies (AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT) have also announced a combined revenue 
backlog of $1.7 trillion, which provides line of sight into these companies’ ability to meet 
required revenue targets.

### Page 7

The magnitude of capex in 2026 and 2027 points to even larger “required” annual 
revenues in the next few years for the “application layer” of companies buying 
much of that compute from the hyperscalers. These revenues reflect the additional 
costs and profit margins of application companies on top of the “required” hyperscaler 
revenues selling compute. EBIT margins for large-cap software companies have 
averaged 40% during the past five years. We utilize EBIT margins rather than gross 
margins to include the costs of both training and inference, and assume additional 
expenses alongside compute. The range of potential required revenues is wide 
depending on which companies constitute the application layer and how their costs are 
structured.  
Exhibit 11 provides a range of potential “required” revenues based on hyperscaler 
ROIC and application layer EBIT margins. For example, the application layer would 
have to generate over $1 trillion in annual revenues if those companies had a 30% EBIT 
margin and the hyperscalers generated a 10%-20% return on invested capital. 
The “required” application layer revenues that would justify current capex are large 
but potentially achievable. For example, estimates of global advertising and software 
spending each equal roughly $1.5 trillion in 2026. Our economists estimate that total 
annual AI US labor productivity gains that will potentially accrue to capital total roughly 
$1.8 trillion. However, not all of these gains will necessarily accrue to the infrastructure 
and application layer companies. In addition, another year of strong capex growth in 
2028 would raise the hurdle further. AI-related revenues and capex needs would be 
substantially larger accounting for physical AI or the potential emergence of AGI.
